where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x{\color[rgb]{0,0,0},}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
requires showing, for arbitrary
s\in\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}{}_{A}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
, that

\displaystyle\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N]a.\,a\langle x\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))~>_{\mathbb{N}}~\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))\ .

Unfolding definitions, we must show

\displaystyle 2+n+\lfloor\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\rfloor

\displaystyle>_{\mathbb{N}}~\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))

\displaystyle\textup{where }(n,t)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{x\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\}}(s)\ .

Applying the Substitution Lemma
21
,

\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{x\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\}}=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v},

we see
(n,t)=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)
.
Indeed, the required result follows immediately from observing that
n=\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))
.

The application, function case,

\displaystyle{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N]a.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~\mathrel{\hbox to10.5pt{\vbox to4.5pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.25pt\lower-2.25pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{{}}{}

{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{7.5pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.07 0 L 0.35 2.4 L 0 0 L 0.35 -2.4 Z}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N]a.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}{}_{A}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}{}_{A}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},

requires showing, for arbitrary
s\in\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}{}_{A}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
, that

\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N]a.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))~>_{\mathbb{N}}~\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N]a.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))\ .

Unfolding definitions, we must show

\displaystyle 1+n+\lfloor\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\rfloor

\displaystyle>_{\mathbb{N}}1+n^{\prime}+\lfloor\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\rfloor

\displaystyle\textup{where }(n,t)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s,a(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}))

\displaystyle\textup{and }(n,^{\prime}t^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s,a(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}))\ .

Applying the inductive hypothesis on
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\mathrel{\hbox to10.5pt{\vbox to4.5pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.25pt\lower-2.25pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{
}{}{}{{}}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 10.2 0}{fill:none} {{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{
{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{7.5pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.07 0 L 0.35 2.4 L 0 0 L 0.35 -2.4 Z}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, we achieve

\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{w}(s,a(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v})))>_{\mathbb{N}}\pi_{1}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{w}(s,a(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v})))\ ,

and thus that
n>_{\mathbb{N}}n^{\prime}
, as required.

\displaystyle{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~\mathrel{\hbox to10.5pt{\vbox to4.5pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.25pt\lower-2.25pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{{}}{}

In the special case where
s
is the minimal element, we recover
\lfloor\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\rfloor>_{\mathbb{N}}\lfloor\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\rfloor
.
Additionally, by Lemma
22
, we have that
\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket\geq_{\mathbb{N}\times\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket}\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
. This allows us to apply monotonicity of

to deduce that
(n,t)\geq_{\mathbb{N}\times\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket}(n^{\prime},t^{\prime})
.
Altogether, this suffices for the result.

\displaystyle=v({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})(r)

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\mathrel{\hbox to10.5pt{\vbox to4.5pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.25pt\lower-2.25pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{{}}{}

Appendix B
Proofs for Section
6
: Categorical Semantics

We formally define the category
\Lambda S/{\sim}
of sequential
\lambda
-terms generated by a set of base types
\Sigma
, modulo an equivalence
\sim
as follows.

Definition 40
.

The category
\Lambda S/{\sim}
is given by:
objects
are type vectors
{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, and a
morphism
from
\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma}
to
\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}
is an equivalence class under
\sim
of closed sequential
\lambda
-terms
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. (Diagrammatic) composition is sequencing
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\,{;}\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
with identity
\mathsf{id}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Open terms are included in the category by closing them:

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\quad\mapsto\quad{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\,\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~.

We will vary the equivalence
\sim
throughout this section.

B.1
A pre-monoidal category

Theorem 41

(Proposition
25
restatement)
.

Terms modulo
\beta\eta
-equivalence form a strict premonoidal category.

Proof.

The associativity and unitality morphisms are identities on type vectors. It remains to show that the left action
-\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\otimes$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\otimes$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\otimes$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\otimes$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}
and right action
\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\otimes$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\otimes$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\otimes$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\otimes$}}}}\kern 1.0pt-
are functorial. Let
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, and let
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. For the left action:

\mathsf{id}_{\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma}}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}~=~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~=~\mathsf{id}_{\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.36165pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}}

\displaystyle(M\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau});(N\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau})

\displaystyle=~({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\,;\,({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})

\displaystyle=~({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})

\displaystyle=_{\beta}\;({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})

\displaystyle=~(M;N)\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\ .

For the right action:

\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\mathsf{id}_{\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma}}~=~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~=_{\eta}~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~=~\mathsf{id}_{\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.36165pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma}}

\begin{aligned} &(\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0ptM);(\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0ptN)\\
&\quad=~({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\,;\,({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\\
&\quad=~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\,{;}\,N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\\
&\quad=~\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt(M;N)\ .\end{aligned}\qed

B.2
A Cartesian closed category

We now take
\sim
to be the equational theory defined in Section 6 and show that
\Lambda S/{\sim}
is in fact a Cartesian closed category.

Remark 42
.

For the
\eta
-law, taking
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}(\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}){\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}(\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
in
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[[x].\,P.\,\langle z\rangle.\,z]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
results in
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[[x].\,\langle z\rangle.\,z]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[x]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
at higher type, so
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[x]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
holds for all types. Iterating results in
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. We will freely use this as a case of the
\eta
-law without mention.

Theorem 43

(Theorem
28
restatement)
.

The category
\Lambda S/{\sim}
is a strict Cartesian closed category.

Proof.

Consider the equipment defined in Section 6.
To prove we have a Cartesian category, we show existence of a terminal object and existence and uniqueness of products.
Terminality of the unit object (the empty type vector) follows from the terminality (
!
) equation.
We define the pairing of terms
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
as

\displaystyle[{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]~\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle\Delta}}{{=}}~\Delta;({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\times{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\ \,{;}\,\ M.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{z}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{z}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

To verify the existence of the product, we show that

\displaystyle[{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}]\ ;\pi_{1}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\ \,{;}\,\ M.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{z}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{z}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\ \,{;}\,\ \langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{z}{}^{\prime}\rangle.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

For the closed structure, we show the existence and uniqueness of exponents. We define the currying of a term
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
as

\displaystyle{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}^{*}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle\Delta}}{{=}}_{\eta}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}(\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\ ,

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
To verify the existence and uniqueness, respectively, of exponents, we show that

\displaystyle(\textsf{id}_{\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho}}\times{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}^{*})\ ;\ \epsilon

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\,{;}\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle k\rangle.\,k\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\beta}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\eta}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

\displaystyle((\textsf{id}_{\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma}}\times{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\ ;\ \epsilon)^{*}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,N.\,\langle k\rangle.\,k]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\epsilon}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

for
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}k\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.

The associators and unitors are given by identities and so are trivially natural isomorphisms which satisfy the coherence diagrams. This fact also makes the category a
strict
Cartesian closed category.
∎

Note that then the canonical morphisms can be recovered from the definition of products and currying above as

\Delta=\langle\textsf{id},\textsf{id}\rangle,\pi_{1}=\mathsf{id}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt{!},\pi_{2}={!}\kern 1.0pt{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\times$}}}}\kern 1.0pt\mathsf{id},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\times{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\langle\pi_{1};{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},\pi_{2};{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rangle,\sigma=\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle,\eta=\textsf{id}^{*}
,
etc
.

We proceed to show that the canonical functor induced by the soundness theorem is in fact faithful.

B.3
Completeness

First, we introduce the simply typed
\lambda
-calculus with patterns, which we use for our translation.

Definition 44
.

The
\lambda
-calculus (with
n
-ary tuples and patterns) generated by a signature
\Sigma
is given by the following grammar:

\displaystyle M,N

\displaystyle~=~x\mid~MN~\mid~\lambda p.M~\mid~(M_{1},\ldots,M_{n})

\displaystyle p,q,r,s,t

\displaystyle~=~x~|~(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})

where from left to right the term constructors are a variable, application, abstraction over a pattern and product. A product is a vector of terms and a pattern is a vector of variables. We freely allow coercion from patterns to terms. Terms are considered modulo
\alpha
-equivalence. We write products, contexts and patterns as vectors
(s_{1},\ldots,s_{n})
and elide the isomorphisms for associativity and unitality so that concatenation of
s
and
t
may be written as
(s\cdot t)
.

Definition 45
.

Simple types are given by the following grammar

\displaystyle A,B~\Coloneqq~\alpha\in\Sigma~\mid~{A}\to{B}~\mid~A\times B

A judgement
\Gamma\vdash M:A
is a typed term in a context
\Gamma=x_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,x_{n}:A_{n}
, a finite function from variables to types.
The typing rules for the simply-typed
\lambda
-calculus (STLC) are given in Figure
2
.

Definition 46
.

The equational theory of the STLC is the least equivalence generated by the following laws, closed under any context:

Beta (Function):

\displaystyle(\lambda p.M)N

\displaystyle\to_{\beta}M\{N/p\}

\displaystyle\textup{Eta (Function): }\textup{x}\not\in\mathsf{fv}(M)

\displaystyle\lambda(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n}).M(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})

\displaystyle=_{\eta}M:A\to B\

Eta (Product):

\displaystyle(\pi_{1}(M),\ldots,\pi_{n}(M))

\displaystyle=_{\eta}M:{A}_{1}\times\ldots\times{A}_{n}

where, in the first case,
\Gamma,x:A\vdash M:B
and
\Gamma\vdash N:A
, and where

\{(N_{1},\ldots,N_{n})/(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n})\}=\{N_{1}/p_{1}\}\ldots\{N_{n}/p_{n}\}

denotes simultaneous substitution, and, in the last case, we define the syntactic sugar
\pi_{i}=\lambda(x_{1},\ldots,x_{n}).x_{i}
.

\begin{array}[]{cc}\hskip 20.00003pt{p_{1}:{A_{1}}\ldots,p_{n}:{A_{n}}\vdash{p_{i}}:{A_{i}}}\end{array}

{\Gamma,(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n}):{A_{1}}\times\ldots\times{A_{n}}\vdash M:{C}}{\Gamma,p_{1}:{A_{1}},\ldots,p_{n}:{A_{n}}\vdash M:{C}}\qquad{\Gamma\vdash(M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}):{A_{1}}\times\ldots\times{A_{n}}}{\{\Gamma\vdash M_{i}:{A_{i}}}\}_{i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}}

\begin{array}[]{cc}\hskip 10.00002pt{\Gamma\vdash MN:{B}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\Gamma\vdash M:{A}}{\Gamma\vdash N:{A}\to{B}}&\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 10.00002pt{\Gamma\vdash\lambda p.M:{A}\to{B}}{\Gamma,p:{A}\vdash M:{B}}\end{array}

Figure 2:
Typing rules for the
\lambda
-calculus with patterns

Note that in this calculus, the
\beta
-law for products is implemented by
\beta
-reduction.

We present the free functor induced by Theorem
28
as a map from FMC terms to lambda terms, defined by induction on type derivations. Let
\textsf{CCC}(\Sigma)
denote the free Cartesian closed category generated by a signature
\Sigma
of base types.

Lemma 47
.

The interpretation functor
\{-\}:\textup{{CCC}}({\Sigma})\to\Lambda S/{\sim}
can equivalently be defined inductively on
\lambda
-terms as follows. On types and contexts, respectively, define:

\displaystyle\{{\alpha}\}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\alpha\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},\textup{ where }{\alpha}\in\Sigma

\displaystyle\{{A}\to{B}\}

\displaystyle=\{{A}\}{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{{B}\}

\displaystyle\{A_{1}\times\ldots\times A_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\{A_{1}\}\ldots\{A_{n}\}

\displaystyle\{A_{1},\ldots,A_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\{A_{n}\}\ldots\{A_{1}\}

Inductively on the type derivation of
\Gamma\vdash M:A
, define an FMC term

by

\displaystyle\{p_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,p_{n}:A_{n}\vdash p_{i}:A_{i}\}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle a_{n}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,\ldots.\,\langle a_{1}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[a_{i}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},

\displaystyle\{\Gamma\vdash(M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}):A_{1}\times\ldots\times A_{n}\}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{\Gamma\vdash M_{1}:A_{1}\}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\ldots\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{\Gamma\vdash M_{n}:A_{n}\}

\displaystyle\{\Gamma,(p_{1},\ldots,p_{n}):A_{1}\times\ldots\times A_{n}\vdash M:B\}

\displaystyle=\{\Gamma,p_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,p_{n}:A_{n}\vdash M:B\}

\displaystyle\{\Gamma\vdash MN:B\}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{\Gamma\vdash N:A\}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}.\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

\displaystyle\qquad{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{\Gamma\vdash M:A\to B\}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}.\,\langle k\rangle.\,k\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

\displaystyle\{\Gamma\vdash\lambda p.M:A\to B\}

\displaystyle={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{\Gamma,p:A\vdash M:B\}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

where in each case
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:\{\Gamma\}
,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}a_{i}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:\{A_{i}\}
, and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}k\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:\{A\to B\}
.

Proof.

We present below the equivalence between the simply typed
\lambda
-calculus and the free Cartesian closed category over the signature
\Sigma
. Using the functor from
CCC
(
\Sigma
) to
\Lambda S/{\sim}
described Theorem
32
allows us to translate the right-hand sides to FMC terms, giving the result.
In the following,
\langle M,N\rangle
denotes the pairing of morphisms, given by
\Delta;(M\times N)
.

\displaystyle\{p_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,p_{n}:A_{n}\vdash p_{i}:A_{i}\}

\displaystyle=\pi_{i},

\displaystyle\{\Gamma\vdash(M_{1},\ldots,M_{n}):A_{1}\times\ldots\times A_{n}\}

\displaystyle=\langle\{\Gamma\vdash M_{1}:A_{1}\},\ldots,\{\Gamma\vdash M_{n}:A_{n}\}\rangle

\displaystyle\{\Gamma,(x_{1},\ldots,x_{n}):A_{1}\times\ldots\times A_{n}\vdash M:B\}

\displaystyle=\{\Gamma,x_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,x_{n}:A_{n}\vdash M:B\}

\displaystyle\{\Gamma\vdash MN:B\}

\displaystyle=\langle\{\Gamma\vdash N:A\},\{\Gamma\vdash M:A\to B\}\rangle\ ;\ \epsilon

\displaystyle\{\Gamma\vdash\lambda p.M:A\to B\}

\displaystyle=\{\Gamma,p:A\vdash M:B\}^{*}\ .\qed

We construct a functor
\llbracket-\rrbracket:\Lambda S/{\sim}\to\textup{{CCC}}(\Sigma)
which we will show is the left-inverse of the interpretation
\{-\}:\textup{{CCC}}({\Sigma})\to\Lambda S/{\sim}
given above.
We first introduce a
valuation
, which interprets the free variables of a term.

Definition 48

(Valuation)
.

A valuation
v
is a function assigning to each FMC-variable
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
a
\lambda
-term
v({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\in\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
.
Given a valuation
v
, let
v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow N\}
denote the valuation which assigns
N
to
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and otherwise behaves as
v
.

The definition of
\llbracket-\rrbracket:\Lambda S/{\sim}\to\textup{{CCC}}(\Sigma)
follows the stack machine intuition of the FMC. The context of the corresponding lambda term represents the input stack and the lambda term itself represents the state of the stack at the end of the run, as a function of the input stack.
The top-level arrow
{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
becomes sequent entailment
\vdash
and the type of the input stack becomes the type of the
\lambda
-context and the type of the output stack becomes the type of the
\lambda
-term.

Theorem 49

(Definition
29
restatement)
.

For every signature
\Sigma
, define the interpretation
\llbracket-\rrbracket:\Lambda S/{\sim}\to\textup{{CCC}}(\Sigma)
inductively on types as:

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\alpha\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\alpha\textup{ where }\alpha\in\Sigma

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau_{1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\ldots\tau_{n}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau_{1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket\times\ldots\times\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau_{n}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket

Lemma 50
.

For all terms
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Gamma}}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Gamma}}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{N}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and valuation

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\cdot r)=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow r\}}(s)\quad{and}\quad\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{N}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{N}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}),

where
{r}:\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
and
{s}:\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

By induction on the sizes of vectors
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{N}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, respectively.
∎

Lemma 51

(Weakening)
.

For all terms
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, valuations
v
and
s\in\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\sigma\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
, we have that

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Gamma}}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Gamma}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0},}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}x~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow s\}},

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\sigma\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\not\in\mathsf{fv}({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})
.

Proof.

Induction on the type derivation of
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
∎

Lemma 52

(Sequencing)
.

For all terms
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and for all valuations
v
, we have that:

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\,{;}\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}({s}\cdot{r})

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}({s}\cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}({r}))

Proof.

We proceed by induction on the type derivation of
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
See Lemma
20
for a similar proof.
∎

Lemma 53

(Substitution)
.

For every pair of terms
\Gamma,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\omega\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\omega\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and for every valuation
v
, we have

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\}}\ .

Proof.

We proceed by induction on the type derivation of
\Gamma\vdash{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
See Lemma
21
for a similar proof.
∎

Lemma 54

(Well-Definedness)
.

For any closed terms
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Gamma}}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Gamma}}~{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and valuation
v
, we have

\displaystyle{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\textsf{eqn}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\quad{implies}\quad\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\ .

Proof.

We prove the statement in the case of each equation, making free use of the Sequencing and Weakening lemmas.

•

Beta:

,

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v})=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\}}(s)

Substitution

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x{\color[rgb]{0,0,0},}~\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.

Interchange:
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\iota}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,N.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\cdot r)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow r\}}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}({s}))

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}({s})\cdot r)

\displaystyle=(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}({s})\cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r))

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.

•

Diagonal:
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\to_{\Delta}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)\}}()

\displaystyle=(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)\cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow{s}\}}()

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow{s}\}}(s)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow{s}\}}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)\cdot s)

∎

Lemma 55
.

The interpretation
\llbracket-\rrbracket:\Lambda S/{\sim}\to\textup{{CCC}}(\Sigma)
is a Cartesian closed functor.

Proof.

Functoriality follows immediately from Lemma
20
(Sequencing). Unitality follows from observing
\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}=\textsf{id}
.
To show
\llbracket-\rrbracket
is a
Cartesian
functor, we must show it commutes with the product functor
\times
.
Making free use of the Sequencing and Weakening lemmas, we have:

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\times{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\cdot r)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N.\,\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\cdot r)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s\ \cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r))

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M.\,[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r)\}}(s)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r)\}}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r)\}}(s))

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r)\}}(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s))

\displaystyle=(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(s)\cdot\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(r))

\displaystyle=(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\times\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket)_{v}(s\cdot r)\ ,

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{u}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
s\in\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
,
r\in\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
.
To show
\llbracket-\rrbracket
is a Cartesian
closed
functor, we must further show that it preserves the arrow functor
\to
. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\to{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(f)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[M.\,x.\,N]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}(f)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[M.\,x.\,N]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow f\}}(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M.\,x.\,N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow f\}}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow f\}}\ ;\ \llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow f\}}\ ;\ \llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow f\}}

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\ ;\ f\ ;\ \llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}

\displaystyle=(\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v}\to\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket_{v})(f)

where
f:\llbracket{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\rrbracket
,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
∎

We demonstrate completeness by proving
\llbracket\{\Gamma\vdash M\}\rrbracket_{v}=\Gamma\vdash M:A
.

Theorem 56

(Theorem
30
restatement)
.

The functor
\{-\}:\textup{{CCC}}(\Sigma)\to\Lambda S/{\sim}
is faithful.

Proof.

We show that
\llbracket\{\Gamma\vdash M:A\}\rrbracket_{v}=\Gamma\vdash M:A
, or equivalently that

\displaystyle\llbracket\{a_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,a_{n}:A_{n}\vdash M:B\}\rrbracket_{v}(a_{n}\cdots a_{1})=M:B\ .

Note we confuse between the input variables
a_{i}
and variables of the context of
M
in order to avoid proliferation of
\alpha
-conversions.
We proceed by induction on the type derivation of
\Gamma\vdash M:A
.

•

The base (variable) case:

\displaystyle\llbracket\{a_{1}:A_{1},\ldots,a_{n}:A_{n}\vdash a_{i}:A_{i}\}\rrbracket_{v}(a_{n}\cdots a_{1})

\displaystyle=\llbracket{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\ldots[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{c\vdash M_{n}\}\rrbracket_{\{{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\leftarrow{c}\}}(M_{1})
