Weakening + IH

\displaystyle=(M_{1},\ldots,M_{n})

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\{\Gamma\}
and
M_{i}:A_{i}
. ∎

We now show that terms modulo machine equivalence also form a Cartesian closed category.
First, we show that machine equivalence is well-defined.

Proposition 57
.

Machine equivalence
(\sim)
is a congruence.

Proof.

It is shown that
(\sim)
is closed under contexts. Reflexivity then follows by induction on the term, symmetry is immediate, and transitivity follows by induction on types.

•

Unit case.
To show is that
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. This requires that

\forall\,T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}.~T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

which is tautologous.

•

Variable case.
To show is that if
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x.\,M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}x.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let both be typed in the context
\Gamma={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, and assume this includes
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let the following stacks be equivalent.

R\sim R^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\qquad T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\qquad W\sim W^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

It must be shown that
U\sim U^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
for the following stacks
U
and
U^{\prime}
.

\displaystyle U

\displaystyle=(TR,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}x.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle U^{\prime}

\displaystyle=(T^{\prime}R^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}x.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

Let
W
and
W^{\prime}
respectively contain the terms
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
corresponding to
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
in
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Then
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}x.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N.\,\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}x.\,M\}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}.\,\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let
(R,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}=S
and
(R^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}=S^{\prime}
, for which the assumption
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
gives
S\sim S^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Then

\begin{array}[]{l@{}c@{}r@{}c@{}r}U&~=&(TR,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N.\,\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}&~=&(TS,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}\\
U^{\prime}&~=&(T^{\prime}R^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}.\,\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}&~=&(T^{\prime}S^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}\end{array}

by composition of machine runs. By the premise
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then
U\sim U^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\upsilon\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
as required.

•

Application case.
To show is that given
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N].\,M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}[N^{\prime}].\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let all four terms be typed in the context
\Gamma={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and let the following stacks be equivalent.

S\sim S^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\qquad W\sim W^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

It must be shown that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
for the following stacks
T
and
T^{\prime}
.

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=~(S,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}[N].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle T^{\prime}

\displaystyle=~(S^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}[N^{\prime}].\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

For
T
, by the definition of substitution and by a single
push
step of the machine,

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=~(S,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}N].\,\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle=~(S\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

and similarly
T^{\prime}=(S^{\prime}\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}
. Since
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then also

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. By the premise
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
it follows that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.

•

Abstraction case.
To show is that if
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then it follows that

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}\langle x\rangle.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
be typed in the context
\Gamma={\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
(not including
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
), and assume the following equivalent stacks and terms.

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\qquad S\sim S^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\qquad W\sim W^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

It must be shown that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
for the following stacks
T
and
T^{\prime}
.

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=~(S\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}\langle x\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle T^{\prime}

\displaystyle=~(S^{\prime}\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}\langle x\rangle.\,M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

By a single
pop
step of the machine,

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=~(S,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N/x,W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle T^{\prime}

\displaystyle=~(S^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{N^{\prime}/x,W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

so that
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
gives
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. ∎

Proposition 58
.

Machine equivalence includes
\beta\eta
-equivalence:
(=_{\beta\eta})\subseteq(\sim)
.

Proof.

For beta, let

, and let both terms be typed in context
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
with type
{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let

and
W\sim W^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. It must be shown that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
for
T
and
T^{\prime}
as follows.

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=~(S,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}[N].\,\langle x\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle T^{\prime}

\displaystyle=~(S^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

Two steps of the machine on
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[N].\,\langle x\rangle.\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
evaluate the redex, to give the following.

T~=~(S,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}\{N/x\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

Then by reflexivity,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, it follows that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Since
\sim
is a congruence, if
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\mathrel{\hbox to10.5pt{\vbox to4.5pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.25pt\lower-2.25pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{
}{}{}{{}}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 10.2 0}{fill:none} {{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{
{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{7.5pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.07 0 L 0.35 2.4 L 0 0 L 0.35 -2.4 Z}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.

For expansion, let
({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})=_{\eta}({\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[x].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})
where
x
is not free in
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, and let both terms be typed in the context
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. Let
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\rho\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,
S\sim S^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, and
W\sim W^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\omega\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. It must be shown that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
for
T
and
T^{\prime}
as follows.

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=~(S\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

\displaystyle T^{\prime}

\displaystyle=~(S^{\prime}\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}\langle x\rangle.\,[x].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

Two steps of the machine on
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,[x].\,M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
pop then push back
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
on the stack, which gives the following.

T^{\prime}~=~(S^{\prime}\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\{W^{\prime}/\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{w}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}){\Downarrow}

Then by reflexivity,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, it follows that
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. As with reduction, since
\sim
is a congruence, if
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\eta}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
then
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M{\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sim\,}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
∎

We next establish that machine equivalence contains the equational theory of the FMC.

Theorem 59

(Theorem
32
restatement)
.

For all typed, closed FMC terms
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, we have that

\displaystyle{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\textsf{eqn}}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\ \qquad\textup{implies}\qquad{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\sim{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\tau\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}.

Proof.

We verify for each equation generating
=_{\textsf{eqn}}
, which suffices since by Proposition
57
, machine equivalence
\sim
is closed under all contexts. In the following, we write
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{S}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
to represent the vector of terms in a stack
S
, and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{S}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
to represent a corresponding sequence of applications (similar to the notation for
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{x}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
). Beta and (first-order) eta-laws are given by the previous proposition.

•

Interchange:

for
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}M\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}

Eta (Higher-order):

{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=_{\epsilon}{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}(\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\ ,

where
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}(\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\colon\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
We have that for all stacks
S\sim S^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\sigma\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,

\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{~,}r@{}}(~S&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\rangle.\,[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{y}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~\epsilon&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{S}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{S}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\end{array}\qquad\textup{and}\qquad\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{~,}r@{}}(~S^{\prime}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\end{array}\,,

where
(S^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\Downarrow{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
.
We thus require to show that
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[S].\,N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\sim{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
. To verify this, observe that for all stacks
R\sim R^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,

\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{~,}r@{}}(~R&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}[\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{S}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}].\,N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~R{S}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N.\,\langle x\rangle.\,x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~R\cdot{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\langle x\rangle.\,x\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~R&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~T&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\end{array}\qquad\textup{and}\qquad\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{~,}r@{}}(~R^{\prime}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\\
\hline\cr(~{T^{\prime}}&~{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}\star\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}~)\end{array}\,,

where
(S,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\Downarrow{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
,
(R,{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\Downarrow T
and
(R^{\prime},{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}})\Downarrow T^{\prime}
. By reflexivity, we have that
{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}\sim{\vphantom{(}\color[rgb]{0.8,0,0}P^{\prime}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\leftharpoonup}{\rho\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}{\kern 1.0pt\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.86108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\raise 0.51663pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Rightarrow$}}}\kern 1.0pt}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
and thus
T\sim T^{\prime}:{\vphantom{)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\kern 0.4pt\accentset{\rightharpoonup}{\tau\kern 0.4pt}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.8}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}
, and the result follows. ∎
